RODS closes the loop between RL training and data generation by repurposing the progress reward variance as a practical, zero-cost boundary detector that requires no extra inference beyond the rollouts already computed for training. It continuously identifies such boundary samples, synthesizes new multi-turn variants matching their structural complexity (e.g., API topology and dependency depth) via a skill-aligned resampling pipeline, and manages a dynamic replay buffer that co-evolves with the policy. Starting from 400 human seeds and maintaining an active training pool of ~800 samples, RODS achieves comparable performance to a 17K-sample offline pipeline while requiring roughly 20x fewer trajectories, and improves over fixed-data RL and environment augmentation in our controlled setting.

</details>


### [76] [ARIADNE: Agnostic Routing for Inference-time Adapter DyNamic sElection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19079)
*Enrico Cassano,Michał Brzozowski,Zuzanna Dubanowska,Paolo Mandica,Neo Christopher Chung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARIADNE是一种无需训练、与适配器无关的推理时动态适配器选择框架，通过在嵌入空间中基于质心距离进行路由，无需访问适配器内部结构或额外训练，在23个NLP任务上恢复了97.44%的上限性能，在44个任务上达到89.7%的平均选择准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PEFT模型生态系统在推理时面临无任务标签的查询，需要自动从不断增长的异构适配器池中选择最合适的适配器。而现有路由方法要么依赖适配器内部结构（如权重分解、梯度统计），要么需要额外训练路由器，限制了可扩展性和可移植性。

Method: ARIADNE为每个适配器计算其训练集嵌入的质心集合，以捕捉该适配器的数据分布；对于无标签输入，在潜在空间中测量其与各质心的距离来选择适配器。路由完全在输入嵌入空间中进行，与任意PEFT方法兼容，无需修改适配器或训练过程。

Result: 在Llama 3.2 1B Instruct上评估23个NLP任务，恢复了97.44%的上限性能；扩展到44个任务时，实现了89.7%的平均选择准确率，且无需额外训练或访问适配器内部结构。

Conclusion: ARIADNE提供了一种可扩展、可移植的推理时适配器选择方案，有效解决了PEFT生态系统中无标签查询的适配器自动选择问题。

Abstract: The increasing deployment of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has led to model ecosystems in which a single backbone is paired with many task-specialized adapters. In this setting, inference-time queries often arrive without task labels, requiring the system to automatically select the most appropriate adapter from a growing and heterogeneous adapter pool. Existing routing methods either depend on access to adapter internals, such as weight decompositions or gradient-based statistics, or require additional router training, which limits scalability and portability as new adapters are added. We introduce ARIADNE, a training-free, adapter-agnostic routing framework for dynamic adapter selection at inference time. ARIADNE represents each adapter through a set of centroids computed from embeddings of its training set, capturing the data distribution associated with that adapter. Given an unlabeled input, it selects an adapter by measuring proximity to these centroids in latent space. Because routing is performed entirely in the input embedding space, ARIADNE is compatible with arbitrary PEFT methods and requires no modification to the adapters or training procedures. Primarily evaluated with Llama 3.2 1B Instruct on 23 diverse NLP tasks, ARIADNE recovers 97.44% of the upper bound performance. Scaling to 44 tasks, it achieves 89.7% average selection accuracy, without additional training or access to adapter internals.

</details>


### [77] [Towards an Agent-First Web: Redesigning the Web for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19116)
*Eranga Bandara,Ross Gore,Ravi Mukkamala,Asanga Gunaratna,Safdar H. Bouk,Xueping Liang,Peter Foytik,Abdul Rahman,Sachini Rajapakse,Isurunima Kularathna,Pramoda Karunarathna,Chalani Rajapakse,Ng Wee Keong,Kasun De Zoysa,Tharaka Hewa,Amin Hass,Wathsala Herath,Aruna Withanage,Nilaan Loganathan,Atmaram Yarlagadda,Sachin Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在访问层、经济层和内容层三个层面重新设计互联网架构，以使AI代理成为网络的“一等公民”，并提出了十项设计原则，包括代理继承人类访问权限、基于意图的经济分层、代理文本标记语言(ATML)和加密溯源链等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web被设计为以人类为主要消费者，但AI代理作为人类与Web内容之间的中介迅速崛起，打破了这一持续三十年的基本假设。现有Web通过全面屏蔽、CAPTCHA排斥以及将代理访问视为“提取”而非合法交互的经济模型来抵制代理，亟需对Web的基础社会契约进行重新协商。

Method: 在访问层提出代理继承人类同等访问权限、速率限制、HTTP请求中的代理标识元数据以及面向人类和面向代理的双层内容架构；在经济层提出基于意图的分层框架（代理的经济义务镜像其代表的人类）、基于代币的订阅计量模型和委托内容经济；在内容层提出代理文本标记语言(ATML)四级人类监督层级模型和加密溯源链以应对认知递归问题。

Result: 形成了面向代理优先互联网的十项设计原则，涵盖访问、经济和内容三个层面，旨在使代理成为Web的一等公民。

Conclusion: Web需要在不破坏其开放性的前提下，通过重新设计访问、经济和内容层面的基础机制，将AI代理有机整合到Web生态中。这要求重新协商Web的基础社会契约，使代理能够作为人类的合法代表而非“提取者”进行交互。

Abstract: The World Wide Web was built on an assumption held for three decades: the primary consumer of web content is a human being. This permeates every layer; its access model presumes human visitors, its economics rest on human attention, and its content targets human perception. The rapid emergence of AI agents as intermediaries between humans and web content invalidates this assumption. Yet the web resists agents through blanket blocking, CAPTCHA-based exclusion, and economic models that treat agent access as extraction rather than legitimate interaction.
  This paper proposes a principled redesign across three layers. At the access layer, agents acting for humans should inherit equivalent access rights, governed by rate limiting and agent identification metadata in HTTP requests, analogous to browser headers, alongside a dual-layer architecture serving human-readable and agent-optimized content from the same domain. At the economic layer, we propose an intent-based tier framework grounded in the agent-as-human-proxy principle: an agent's economic obligation mirrors that of the human it represents. A token-based subscription model meters content in tokens rather than pageviews, alongside a commissioned content economy anchoring AI content production in human intentionality. At the content layer, we identify epistemic recursion, the self-referential loop in which AI-generated content is consumed by agents to produce further content, progressively detaching web knowledge from human ground truth. We propose the Agent Text Markup Language (ATML), a four-level human supervision tier model, and a cryptographic provenance chain to counter this threat.
  Together these constitute ten design principles for an agent-first internet, one in which agents are first-class citizens whose integration requires renegotiating the web's foundational social contract across access, economics, and content.

</details>


### [78] [Analysing drivers and interdependencies in European electricity markets using XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19118)
*Antoine Pesenti,Aidan O'Sullivan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文采用深度神经网络结合可解释人工智能（XAI）技术，对39个欧洲竞标区的电力价格决定因素进行分析。通过SHAP和扩展的SSHAP框架量化特征贡献，发现可再生能源（尤其是太阳能）在价格形成中作用显著，天然气价格仍是主导驱动力，区域互联对价格动态影响重大。此外，构建了全欧盟统一市场的反事实情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场具有强非线性、高维交互和跨区域相互依赖等复杂特性，深度神经网络虽有强预测能力但缺乏可解释性，限制了对其价格形成机制的理解。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用深度神经网络（DNN）进行电价预测，结合SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）量化特征贡献，并应用和扩展SSHAP聚合框架以提升高维设定下的可解释性。同时构建全欧盟统一市场的反事实情景进行对比分析。

Result: 可再生能源（尤其是太阳能）在电力市场中的份额虽低，但对价格形成的作用却异常显著；天然气价格仍是稳定且主导的驱动因素；电网互联显著影响价格动态，体现了欧洲电力系统的高度相互依赖性。

Conclusion: DNN与XAI结合能有效揭示电力市场价格形成的驱动因素；可再生能源的定价影响力被低估；欧洲电力市场的区域互联与一体化对价格具有重要影响；全欧盟统一市场的情景分析为政策制定提供了参考。

Abstract: Electricity markets are inherently complex systems characterised by strong nonlinearities, high-dimensional interactions, and increasing interdependence across regions. While deep neural networks (DNNs) have demonstrated strong predictive capabilities for electricity prices, their lack of interpretability limits their usefulness for understanding the underlying drivers of price formation. This paper addresses this gap by combining DNN models with explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) techniques to analyse the determinants of electricity prices across 39 European bidding zones. We employ SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) to quantify feature contributions and apply and extend SSHAP, an aggregation framework to improve interpretability in high-dimensional settings. The analysis identifies that renewable energy sources, particularly solar, play a disproportionately important role in price formation despite their lower share in total power generation. Gas prices remain a dominant and consistent driver across electricity markets, while interconnections significantly shape price dynamics, highlighting the strong interdependence of European electricity systems. In addition, a synthetic EU-wide electricity market is constructed to explore the counterfactual scenario of a fully integrated market with a single price.

</details>


### [79] [Human-AI Coevolution Dynamics: A Formal Theory of Social Intelligence Emergence Through Long-Term Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19144)
*Jingyi Zhou,Senlin Luo,Haofan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HACD-H框架将人机交互建模为自组织社会认知系统，通过情感适应、关系组织、社会记忆和人格一致性等机制的统一动力学模型，揭示了社会智力源于长期社会认知共演而非孤立对话能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话AI系统在语言生成、个性化和长上下文交互方面取得进展，但大多通过孤立组件（如情感建模、记忆检索、人格条件化）建模社会行为，缺乏统一框架来解释长期人机交互中稳定社会关系和社会智力的涌现。

Method: 提出HACD-H框架，整合情感适应、关系组织、社会记忆和人格一致性为统一动力学模型；引入多时间尺度社会认知、关系吸引子、信任盆地、发展阶段转变和社会认知能量动力学等原理；构建约14,700轮交互的对话数据集，开发理论驱动的经验评估框架。

Result: 发现社会认知的时间持久性层次结构、稳定关系吸引子、类似阶段转变的发展模式、结构化的社会认知能量景观。社会智力与社会认知能量呈显著负相关（r=-0.391, p<0.001），交互轨迹随时间表现出渐进能量减少。

Conclusion: 社会智力源于长期社会认知共演而非孤立对话能力；HACD-H为建模自适应人机社会交互和发展社会智能AI系统提供了统一理论基础。

Abstract: Current conversational AI systems have made significant progress in language generation, personalization, and long-context interaction. However, most existing methods model social behavior through isolated components such as emotion modeling, memory retrieval, or persona conditioning, lacking a unified framework to explain the emergence of stable social relationships and social intelligence in long-term human-AI interaction.To address this, we propose the Human-AI Coevolution Dynamics Framework (HACD-H), a formal model of human-AI interaction as a self-organizing social cognitive system. HACD-H integrates emotional adaptation, relational organization, social memory, and personality consistency into a unified dynamical framework and introduces principles including multi-timescale social cognition, relational attractors, trust basins, developmental phase transitions, and social cognitive energy dynamics.We construct a conversational dataset with approximately 14,700 interaction turns and develop a theory-driven empirical evaluation framework. Results reveal a hierarchy of temporal persistence in social cognition, stable relational attractors, phase-transition-like developmental patterns, and a structured social cognitive energy landscape. Social intelligence shows a significant negative correlation with social cognitive energy (r = -0.391, p < 0.001), and interaction trajectories exhibit progressive energy reduction over time.These findings suggest that social intelligence emerges from long-term social cognitive coevolution rather than isolated conversational capabilities. HACD-H provides a unified theoretical foundation for modeling adaptive human-AI social interaction and developing socially intelligent AI systems.

</details>


### [80] [Beyond Safe Data: Pretraining-Stage Alignment with Regular Safety Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19168)
*Jinhan Li,Kexian Tang,Yihan Xu,Zhuorui Ye,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出安全反思预训练方法，通过在预训练语料中插入安全反思内容，使大语言模型在预训练阶段就建立自我监控能力，超越单纯的数据过滤和改写，有效防范模型从看似安全的数据中学到不安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练阶段的安全对齐主要聚焦于过滤不安全数据或将其改写为更安全的形式，但即使数据本身安全，LLM仍可能将看似良性的知识和能力组合成不安全行为，因此需要更深入的安全对齐方法。

Method: 提出Safety Reflection Pretraining方法，在预训练语料中定期插入简短的安全反思内容，将自我监控直接整合到语言建模中，建立基础安全能力，后续通过兼容的后训练进行强化。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu上预训练的1.7B模型实验表明，该方法提高了安全分类准确率，显著降低了推理阶段和微调攻击的成功率。在合成环境MedSafetyWorld中，相比数据过滤和改写，该方法在防止模型从安全数据泛化出危险行为方面具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 预训练阶段的安全对齐不应仅关注数据安全，还应主动塑造模型从安全数据中可能习得的行为模式，安全反思预训练是实现这一目标的有效方法。

Abstract: To achieve deeper safety alignment for large language models (LLMs), recent efforts have studied how to push safety interventions earlier into the pretraining stage, primarily by filtering unsafe data or rewriting it into safer forms. We argue that pretraining-stage alignment should go beyond making the data safe: LLMs may compose seemingly benign knowledge and capabilities into unsafe behaviors. To this end, we propose Safety Reflection Pretraining, a pretraining-stage alignment method which regularly inserts short safety reflections into pretraining corpora to integrate self-monitoring directly into language modeling, establishing a foundational capability that is subsequently reinforced by compatible post-training. Our experiments with 1.7B models pretrained on FineWeb-Edu show that Safety Reflection Pretraining improves safety classification accuracy and substantially reduces the success rates of inference-stage and finetuning attacks. Complementary to our real-world experiments, we also introduce a fully controlled synthetic environment, MedSafetyWorld, with a clear definition of safety and a reasoning structure under which models can easily generalize unsafe behaviors from safe data. Ablations in MedSafetyWorld further demonstrate a clear advantage of Safety Reflection Pretraining in preventing models from acting on unsafe behaviors generalized from safe data, compared with data filtering and rewriting. Taken together, our findings suggest that pretraining alignment should not only make the training data safe, but also shape the behaviors that models are likely to acquire from safe data.

</details>


### [81] [User as Engram: Internalizing Per-User Memory as Local Parametric Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19172)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出User as Engram方法，将用户事实以手术式编辑存入模型的哈希键控记忆表（Engram），推理技能保留在共享适配器中。相比每用户LoRA适配器，该方法实现5.6倍更高的间接推理准确率，记忆足迹缩小约33,000倍，且编辑可组合、可解释，超过约100个事实后性能超越基于检索的流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型个性化方法存在根本性缺陷：将用户事实写入权重（如每用户LoRA）会污染无关文本，因为其将内容与推理技能折叠进一个全局权重增量中；而将事实存储在权重外（如记忆文件或检索索引）则带来检索开销。受大脑启发（海马体存储稀疏局部记忆痕迹，新皮层存储共享技能），需要一种将内容与推理技能分离的方法。

Method: User as Engram：1）将用户内容以手术式编辑写入Engram模型的哈希键控记忆表，编辑精确触发行查找、添加答案值、保持其他位置不变；2）推理技能保留在一个共享适配器中；3）不同用户事实落在不重叠的哈希槽中，可加性无损叠加。

Result: 1）匹配每用户LoRA的直接召回，间接推理准确率平均提升5.6倍；2）内存足迹缩小约33,000倍；3）编辑是"玻璃盒"操作，可精确验证；4）多个用户可共存于同一共享表；5）超过约100个事实后，性能超越基于检索的2.5倍更大模型流水线。

Conclusion: User as Engram通过将内容与技能分离（手术式编辑存储事实+共享适配器承载推理），实现了比LoRA更优的个性化效果、更小的内存开销和更好的可组合性，且不会降低任何用户的推理能力。

Abstract: Personal memory in a language model is two problems: content and reasoning skill. The brain keeps the two apart (a sparse, local engram in the hippocampus for each episode, a slow neocortex for the shared skills that interpret it), so a new fact need not overwrite everything else. Most personalization today keeps a user's facts outside the weights, in a natural-language memory file or a retrieval index. When facts are written into the model instead, the standard recipe is the per-user LoRA adapter, which does the opposite of the brain, folding content and skill into one global weight delta. Writing a user's facts as a LoRA contaminates text unrelated to them; writing the same facts as local Engram rows leaves it mathematically untouched, resulting in a roughly 33,000x smaller memory footprint.
  We therefore propose User as Engram: store a user's content as surgical edits to the hash-keyed memory table of an Engram model, and carry the reasoning skill in one shared adapter. This layered design matches per-user LoRA's direct recall while delivering 5.6x higher indirect-reasoning accuracy on average, and never makes a single user worse at reasoning than the untouched base. The edit is a glass box: writing a fact switches on its lookup at exactly the trigger, adds the value the answer needs, leaves every other position unchanged to the last bit, and fails if written into the wrong layer. Because different users' facts land in disjoint hash slots, their edits compose: many users live in one shared table at once, stacking additively and losslessly, where a per-user LoRA, a single global weight delta, admits only one. Upon retrieval, a per-user Engram table does not grow with the population the retriever must search, so past ~100 facts it overtakes a retrieval pipeline on a 2.5x larger model.

</details>


### [82] [TxBench-PP: Analyzing AI Agent Performance on Small-Molecule Preclinical Pharmacology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19245)
*Hannah Le,Ramesh Ramasamy,Alex Urrutia,Mahsa Yazdani,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TxBench-PP，一个用于评估AI智能体在小分子临床前药理学决策中表现的可验证基准测试。研究表明，即使是最好的模型配置（Claude Opus 4.8/Pi）也仅通过59.3%的测试端点，表明当前AI系统无法可靠地恢复临床前药理学决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体有望加速药物发现，但在实际项目决策中的可信评估缺失。现有基准测试多依赖文献记忆而非真实实验数据分析，缺乏可验证的、覆盖临床前药理学各环节的标准化评估框架。

Method: 构建包含100个评估任务的TxBench-PP基准测试，按项目阶段、分析类型和任务结构索引，涵盖MoA、PD推理、化合物-靶点结合、因果靶点验证、可开发性与安全性、转化疗效六类任务。智能体在编码环境中检查真实工作流快照文件，返回结构化答案并由确定性评分系统自动评判。

Result: 在16个模型-框架配置、11个模型和4800条轨迹中，最佳配置Claude Opus 4.8/Pi通过59.3%（178/300, 95% CI 51.1-67.6），GPT-5.5/Pi通过55.3%（166/300, 95% CI 47.0-63.6），无系统能可靠恢复临床前药理学决策。

Conclusion: 当前AI智能体在临床前药理学决策任务上表现不足，TxBench-PP为评估和推动AI在药物发现中的实际部署提供了可扩展的基准框架。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) agents promise to accelerate drug discovery by compressing interpretation and decision-making loops, but practical deployment requires trusted evaluation on realistic program decisions. We introduce TherapeuticsBench Preclinical Pharmacology (TxBench-PP), a verifiable benchmark for small-molecule preclinical pharmacology and the first focused slice of a broader TherapeuticsBench effort across drug-discovery stages and therapeutic modalities. TxBench-PP tests whether agents can recover accurate conclusions from real-world assay data rather than memorized facts from literature. The benchmark contains 100 evaluations indexed by program stage, assay type, and task structure, spanning mechanism-of-action (MoA) and pharmacodynamic (PD) reasoning, compound-target engagement, causal target validation, developability and safety, and translational efficacy. Agents receive realistic workflow snapshots, inspect files in a coding environment, and return structured answers graded deterministically. Across 16 model-harness configurations, comprising 11 models and 4,800 trajectories, no system reliably recovered preclinical pharmacology decisions. The strongest configuration, Claude Opus 4.8 / Pi, passed 59.3\% of endpoint attempts (178/300; 95\% CI, 51.1-67.6), followed by GPT-5.5 / Pi at 55.3\% (166/300; 47.0-63.6).

</details>


### [83] [X+Slides: Benchmarking Audience-Conditioned Slide Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19256)
*Haodong Chen,Xuanhe Zhou,Wei Zhou,Xinyue Shao,Yanbing Zhu,Bo Wang,Jiawei Hong,Anya Jia,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了X+Slides基准，用于评估面向目标受众的幻灯片自动生成。通过构建8,133个源验证探针和受众特定效用权重，从受众覆盖度、领域覆盖度、效率和正确性四个维度评估现有系统，发现当前系统虽能恢复部分受众关键信息，但视觉质量和主题覆盖面不能替代源验证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻灯片生成基准仅评估完整性和技术深度，忽略了目标受众这一关键现实因素（如专家需要严格证明，决策者需要可操作结论）。

Method: 构建覆盖113个主题和7个演示场景的语料库，设计8,133个去重源验证探针，为每个探针分配受众特定的效用权重，提出受众覆盖度、领域覆盖度、效率、正确性四个互补指标。

Result: 在τ_A=0.7阈值下，DeepPresenter受众覆盖度0.714，SlideTailor为0.594，NotebookLM消融实验达0.853但存在明显的源差异。

Conclusion: 视觉质量和广泛主题覆盖不能替代源验证评估；当前系统能恢复大部分但非全部的受众关键信息。

Abstract: Automatically generating slide decks from source documents is an important application of large language models (LLMs). Existing benchmarks primarily assess slide completeness and technical depth, while overlooking the target audience as a critical real-world factor. For instance, specialists demand rigorous proofs, whereas decision-makers prioritize actionable conclusions. To bridge this gap, we introduce X+Slides, a benchmark specifically designed for audience-conditioned slide generation. Built on a diverse corpus spanning 113 topics and seven presentation scenes, X+Slides employs a dynamic evaluation framework constructed from 8,133 deduplicated, source-grounded probes. By assigning audience-specific utility weights to the same source-grounded probes, X+Slides reports four complementary metrics: Audience Coverage measures how much audience-essential information is conveyed, Domain-wise Coverage shows which information types are covered, Efficiency measures delivered utility per unit of attention cost, and Correctness verifies whether slide claims are supported by the source. Experiments on DeepPresenter, SlideTailor, and NotebookLM show that current systems can recover a substantial but still incomplete part of audience-essential information: at $τ_A=0.7$, DeepPresenter reaches a best Audience Coverage of 0.714, SlideTailor reaches 0.594, and the NotebookLM ablation reaches 0.853 while showing clear grounding differences. These results indicate that visual quality and broad topic coverage should not be treated as evidence support without source-grounded evaluation.

</details>


### [84] [NeSyCat Torch: A Differentiable Tensor Implementation of Categorical Semantics for Neurosymbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19279)
*Daniel Romero Schellhorn,Till Mossakowski,Björn Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeSyCat Torch 将神经符号语义统一在单一归纳定义框架下，通过强单子和真值聚合结构参数化，利用分布单子、懒惰对数张量单子和批处理单子实现参考语义、可微训练和高效批处理，在MNIST加法任务上优于LTN和DeepProbLog，接近DeepStochLog，并保持框架通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号语义学存在碎片化问题，经典、模糊、概率和神经系统各自使用不同的归纳规则定义真值。NeSyCat虽然通过强单子和聚合结构统一了这些语义，但缺乏对由神经网络学习的谓词和函数的支持。

Method: 提出NeSyCat Torch作为缺失的桥梁，通过神经网络解释计算符号；使用分布单子提供参考语义和度量评估，懒惰对数张量单子（基于对数半环）实现数值稳定可微训练，批处理单子支持高效批量训练。公理即源代码，用monad-based do-notation编写，monadic bind自动执行边缘化和惰性剪枝。

Result: 在MNIST加法任务上，HaskTorch、JAX和PyTorch实现在速度和准确率上优于LTN和DeepProbLog，准确率接近DeepStochLog。

Conclusion: 该方法保持框架统一性，适用于多种一阶神经符号方法，且构造对单子是参数化的（如实例化为Giry单子可扩展到连续概率），未来工作将探索神经表示。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic semantics is fragmented: classical, fuzzy, probabilistic and neural systems each define truth by their own inductive rules. NeSyCat, extending ULLER, subsumes them under a single inductive definition of truth, parametric in a strong monad and an aggregation structure on truth-values. NeSyCat has so far lacked an account of predicates and functions learned by neural networks.
  We provide NeSyCat Torch as the missing link and interpret computational symbols via neural networks, implementing the framework in probabilistic programming and tensor-based backends. We use the distribution monad for reference semantics and metric evaluation, and complement it by a monad for numerically stable, differentiable training: the lazy log-tensor monad over the log-semiring. For efficient training in batches, we furthermore employ a batch monad. The axioms are the source code: written once in monad-based do-notation, monadic bind performs marginalisation, lazily pruning unneeded branches.
  On MNIST addition, our HaskTorch, JAX, and PyTorch implementations outperform LTN and DeepProbLog in speed and accuracy, while achieving nearly the accuracy of DeepStochLog. However, unlike DeepStochLog, we stay in a uniform framework that applies to many first-order NeSy approaches. Namely, the construction is parametric in the monad; instantiating it with, e.g., the Giry monad extends the approach to continuous probability (working out a neural representation here is left for future work).

</details>


### [85] [Rethinking Reward Supervision: Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19327)
*Siyi Gu,Jialin Chen,Sophia Zhou,Arman Cohan,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation框架，利用结构化细粒度评分标准（rubrics）替代传统的思维链蒸馏和标量奖励学习方法，通过两阶段流程（先生成任务特定rubrics，再训练rubric引导的推理器）实现更精细的推理过程信用分配。在科学推理基准上平均超越GRPO 1.0分、OPSD 0.9分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理语言模型的后训练方法存在两个主要问题：(1) 监督式蒸馏依赖昂贵且可能含有噪声、不完整或部分错误的思维链标注，即使最终答案正确，不完美的推理过程也会干扰学习；(2) 基于可验证奖励的强化学习将评估反馈压缩为标量信号，掩盖了响应中需要改进的具体方面。为了克服这些局限，作者提出使用结构化、细粒度的评分标准来提供更精确的反馈信号。

Method: 提出Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation框架：(1) 教师模型基于标准级别的rubrics，为学生模型自身采样的轨迹提供token级别的指导；(2) 避免将单一参考推理链作为唯一的监督目标，而是通过rubrics明确强响应应满足的条件；(3) 两阶段流程：第一阶段学习生成任务特定rubrics，第二阶段训练rubric引导的推理器；(4) 实现比标量奖励优化更细粒度的推理过程信用分配。

Result: 在多样化科学推理基准上，Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation平均超越GRPO 1.0分，超越OPSD 0.9分，有效将rubric级别的标准转化为推理过程中的token级别指导。

Conclusion: Rubric条件化自蒸馏能够有效克服传统蒸馏和强化学习方法中反馈信号粗糙的问题，通过结构化细粒度评分标准实现更精准的推理过程指导，显著提升了科学推理任务的性能。

Abstract: Post-training of reasoning language models is commonly driven by supervised distillation and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. Distillation often relies on chain-of-thought annotations that are expensive to obtain and may themselves be noisy, incomplete, or partially incorrect; even when the final solution is correct, an imperfect rationale can interfere with learning. Reinforcement learning with verified rewards, on the other hand, typically compresses evaluative feedback into a scalar signal, obscuring which aspects of a response should be improved. We propose \textbf{Rubric-Conditioned Self-Distillation}, a framework that incorporates rubrics as structured, fine-grained feedback for on-policy self-distillation. Our method conditions the teacher model on criterion-level rubrics and uses it to provide token-level guidance on the student's own sampled trajectories. This design avoids treating a single reference rationale as the sole supervision target. Instead, rubrics specify what a strong response should satisfy, enabling more fine-grained credit assignment over the reasoning process than scalar reward optimization. We instantiate this framework with a two-stage pipeline that first learns to generate task-specific rubrics and then trains a rubric-guided reasoner. We evaluate on a diverse suite of science reasoning benchmarks and results show that rubric-conditioned self-distillation effectively converts rubric-level criteria into token-level guidance over the reasoning process, surpassing GRPO by 1.0 points and OPSD by 0.9 points on average.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Conflict-Aware Retriever Editing for Knowledge Injection Attacks on LLM-Based RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18310)
*Xinru Liu,Xianglong Zhang,Di Cai,Zhumin Chen,Pengfei Hu,Xin Xin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CAREATTACK，一种基于模型中心（model-centric）的检索器攻击框架，通过冲突感知的检索器编辑和攻击保持的锚点修复，在RAG系统中注入恶意知识。实验证明该方法能有效将恶意文本推入检索结果，揭示了开源检索模型在RAG系统中的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG注入攻击主要依赖数据中心的恶意语料库操作，但合成的对抗文本容易被检测；而开源检索模型的广泛使用暴露了模型中心攻击的新面。作者旨在探索一种更隐蔽、更有效的模型级攻击方式。

Method: CAREATTACK包含两阶段：1) 冲突感知检索器编辑：将闭式参数编辑适配到稠密检索模型，通过图检测和参数编辑投影解决参数冲突；2) 攻击保持锚点修复：对编辑后的检索器进行轻量校准，消除对非目标提示的影响同时保留攻击效果。

Result: 在Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B和BGE-M3上，基于三个基准数据集的实验表明，该方法能显著提升恶意文本在RAG检索结果中的排名，并支持批量目标提示和文本的攻击。

Conclusion: 由于大多数RAG系统基于开源检索模型构建，该工作揭示了一个实际的攻击面，强调了模型中心安全威胁的重要性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Injecting malicious knowledge into retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can manipulate retrieved evidence and mislead downstream generation, posing a serious security threat for AI applications. Existing RAG injection attacks mainly rely on manipulating external knowledge bases, such as crafting malicious corpus. However, the synthetic text crafted by such data-centric methods could be detectable, leading to the failure of attacks. Beyond corpus manipulation, open-source retrievers are increasingly exposing RAG systems to model-centric attacks. In this paper, we propose conflict-aware retriever editing, i.e., CAREATTACK, a model-centric retriever attack framework for malicious knowledge injection in RAG. Specifically, CAREATTACK consists two stages of conflict-aware retriever editing and attack-preserving anchor repair. Conflict-aware retriever editing adapts efficient closed-form parameter editing to the dense retrieval model, promoting malicious knowledge above benign competing passages and resolving potential parameter conflicts through graph-based conflict detection and parameter editing projection. Then, attack-preserving anchor repair performs lightweight calibration on the edited retriever to further eliminate the impact on non-target prompts while preserving the attack effectiveness for target prompts. We instantiate CAREATTACK on Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B and BGE-M3, and conduct evaluation on three benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate our method substantially promote malicious passages into the retrieved knowledge of RAG systems and can perform attacks for batches of target prompts and passages, given the access of retrieval model parameters. Since most RAG systems are built upon open-source retrieval models, this work reveals a practical attack surface in RAG systems. Codes are public accessible at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CareAttack-3F1C.

</details>


### [87] [TIGER: Inverting Transformer Gradients via Embedding-Subspace Distance Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18312)
*William Kalikman,Ivo Petrov,Dimitar I. Dimitrov,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TIGER是一种针对Transformer模型的连续梯度反转攻击方法，通过将子空间信号转化为可微目标，直接优化token嵌入以最小化其与子空间的距离，在编码器模型上显著提升重建质量和速度，在解码器模型上比现有方法更具鲁棒性，首次实现在差分隐私防御的联邦学习场景下的成功重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度反转攻击方法存在两类问题：一是通过优化虚拟输入匹配真实梯度更新的方法成本高且不稳定；二是基于注意力梯度低秩性的子空间方法在进行离散token成员测试时，对数值噪声（如量化或差分隐私）脆弱，且对非因果注意力的编码器模型扩展性差。

Method: TIGER将子空间信号转化为可微目标函数，不搜索离散token或匹配完整梯度，而是直接优化连续的token嵌入，使其到子空间的距离最小化，从而以连续优化的方式实现梯度反转攻击。

Result: 在编码器模型上，TIGER在重建质量和运行时间上均显著优于现有攻击方法；在解码器模型上，TIGER比现有基于子空间的攻击更具鲁棒性，首次实现在差分隐私保护的联邦学习场景下的成功重建。

Conclusion: TIGER通过连续优化方式解决了现有子空间方法对数值噪声敏感和扩展性差的问题，显著提升了梯度反转攻击在Transformer模型上的效果和鲁棒性，特别是在差分隐私防御环境下仍能实现有效重建。

Abstract: Federated learning allows multiple clients to jointly train a shared model by sending gradient updates to a central server while keeping raw inputs local. However, prior gradient inversion attacks show that these updates can reveal enough information to reconstruct client inputs. Existing attacks on transformers either optimize dummy inputs to match the true client updates, which is costly and unstable for modern models, or exploit the low rank of attention gradients to identify a subspace containing the true layer embeddings, followed by a discrete membership test for candidate tokens. However, this token test is brittle under numerical noise, i.e., from quantization or Differential Privacy (DP), and scales poorly for encoder models with non-causal attention. We introduce TIGER, a continuous gradient inversion attack that turns this subspace signal into a differentiable objective. Instead of searching over tokens or matching full gradients, TIGER directly optimizes token embeddings to minimize their distance to the subspace. Our experiments demonstrate that on encoder-only models, TIGER substantially improves both reconstruction quality and runtime over existing attacks, while on decoder models, TIGER is more robust than prior subspace-based attacks, enabling the first successful reconstructions in DP-defended federated learning settings.

</details>


### [88] [TopVenues: A Reproducible Corpus and Tooling Substrate for Cybersecurity Literature Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18320)
*Sidnei Barbieri,Ágney Lopes Roth Ferraz,Lourenço Alves Pereira Júnior*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TopVenues 是一个开源系统，通过将语料库构建作为版本化的研究工件，为网络安全文献综述提供可复现的分母。该系统使用 DBLP 作为元数据主干，集成多种API获取摘要和BibTeX，存储为单调SQLite快照。2026年5月快照包含来自11个网络安全来源的9,925篇论文，摘要覆盖率99.86%，BibTeX覆盖率99.99%。利用该固定分母可重复测量：29.2%的2024-2025顶级安全会议论文以arXiv预印本形式出现，中位提前5个月；基于作者历史记录的过滤器在90%召回率下实现16.5倍精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决网络安全文献综述中可重复性不足的问题，特别是文献筛选和综合前的论文集合（即"分母"）常从出版商门户、书目索引和学术API中重建，而这些来源的覆盖范围、格式和查询语义随时间变化，导致研究结果难以复现。

Method: 构建TopVenues开源系统：声明研究范围和年份，以DBLP书目数据库为元数据主干，通过开放学术API和特定出版商的提取器丰富论文记录（摘要和BibTeX），将结果存储在单调递增的SQLite快照中，并提供CLI、Web界面和导出路径。系统还包含250项测试验证数据完整性。

Result: 2026年5月快照包含11个网络安全来源（2017-2026年）的9,925篇论文，摘要覆盖率99.86%，BibTeX覆盖率99.99%；全文关键词搜索在31毫秒内完成。利用该分母测量发现：29.2%的2024-2025顶级安全会议论文在arXiv上作为预印本出现，中位提前5个月；作者历史记录过滤器在90%召回率下实现16.5倍的精度提升。

Conclusion: TopVenues通过将语料库构建作为可执行、可检查、可引用的版本化研究工件，实现了可审计的网络安全测量，解决了文献综述的可重复性问题。

Abstract: Cybersecurity literature reviews require a reproducible denominator: the set of papers that a protocol includes before screening and synthesis begin. Today, that denominator is often reconstructed from publisher portals, bibliographic indices, and scholarly application programming interfaces (APIs) whose coverage, formats, and query semantics change over time. This paper presents TopVenues, an open-source system that materializes corpus construction as a versioned research artifact. TopVenues declares a venue and year scope, uses DBLP Computer Science Bibliography (DBLP) as the metadata spine, enriches records with abstracts and BibTeX entries via open scholarly APIs and publisher-specific extractors, and stores the results in a monotonic SQLite snapshot, accessible via a command-line interface (CLI), a web interface, and export paths for review workflows. The May 2026 snapshot contains 9,925 papers from 11 cybersecurity sources over 2017 to 2026, with 99.86% abstract coverage and 99.99% BibTeX coverage; keyword search over the full corpus completes in under 31 ms, and a 250-test suite validates the data-integrity invariants. The fixed denominator also enables repeatable measurement: 29.2% of 2024 to 2025 papers from the four top-ranked security conferences in our scope appear as arXiv preprints, with a median of five months before publication, and a prior-author-track-record filter yields a 16.5x precision gain at 90% recall for triaging preprints that later appear in the same venue set. TopVenues links corpus construction to auditable cybersecurity measurement by making the corpus itself executable, inspectable, and citable. The artifact is available at https://github.com/sidneibarbieri/topVenues.

</details>


### [89] [Agentra: A Supervisable Multi-Agent Framework for Enterprise Intrusion Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18325)
*Raj Patel,Shaswata Mitra,Michele Guida,Stefano Iannucci,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Agentra是一个可监督的多智能体入侵响应系统框架，通过角色分工的智能体协作、规划器-验证器审查循环、安全网关过滤和行动目录风险评分，将IDS/EDR/XDR告警转化为结构化的MITRE ATT&CK/D3FEND/NIST CSF 2.0响应计划，在120事件语料上将FP-aware IRS F1从0.61提升至0.84，并将有害行动率恢复至静态基线的0.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业入侵响应仍依赖静态剧本和分析师手动分类，导致告警生成与响应遏制之间存在延迟，亟需自动化且可审计的响应系统。

Method: 提出Agentra多智能体框架，包含角色限定智能体（分解响应推理）、规划器-验证器审查循环（验证计划）、版主安全网关（筛选威胁情报）、行动目录与风险评分（门控行动）、仅追加审计日志（记录决策），并基于MITRE ATT&CK/D3FEND/NIST CSF 2.0进行知识本体映射。

Result: 在ThreatHunter-Playbook、Splunk BOTSv3和DARPA OpTC的120事件语料上，最优配置将FP-aware IRS F1从0.61提升至0.84，并将有害行动率从仅规划器配置的不安全过度反应恢复至静态基线水平的0.0%。

Conclusion: 多智能体响应规划能够提升基于知识本体的IRS覆盖范围，同时保留分析师审批和可审计性。

Abstract: Enterprise intrusion response still depends on static playbooks and analyst-driven triage, creating delay between alert generation and containment. We present Agentra, a supervisable multi-agent Intrusion Response System (IRS) framework that converts alerts from IDS, EDR, and XDR platforms into structured incident response plans grounded in MITRE ATT&CK, MITRE D3FEND, and NIST CSF 2.0. Agentra decomposes response reasoning across role-scoped agents, validates proposed plans through a bounded Planner--Validator review loop, screens retrieved threat intelligence through a Moderator security gateway, gates actions through an Action Catalog and risk score, and records decisions in an append-only audit log. We evaluate Agentra against a static OASIS CACAO v2.0 cyber-playbook baseline on a 120-event corpus drawn from ThreatHunter-Playbook, Splunk BOTSv3, and DARPA OpTC. The strongest configuration improves FP-aware IRS F1 from 0.61 to 0.84 and restores the projected harmful-action rate to the static baseline level of 0.0% after Planner-only configurations introduce unsafe overreaction. These results indicate that multi-agent response planning can improve ontology-grounded IRS coverage while preserving analyst approval and auditability.

</details>


### [90] [SafeClawBench: Separating Semantic, Audit-Evidence, and Sandbox Harm in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18356)
*Yuchuan Tian,Mengyu Zheng,Haocheng Mei,Ye Yuan,Chao Xu,Xinghao Chen,Hanting Chen,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SafeClawBench 是一个面向工具使用型语言模型智能体的分阶段安全基准测试，包含600个对抗任务和三个评估端点（语义接受度、审计可见危害、沙箱工具/状态危害），揭示了不同评估维度下安全失败模式的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将智能体安全失败的多个阶段（如文本同意、实际工具操作等）合并为单一攻击成功率，无法区分模型仅仅是同意了攻击者的指令还是实际产生了可观察的危害，导致安全评估不精确。

Method: 构建SafeClawBench，包含600个受控对抗任务，跨越6种攻击类型（直接/间接提示注入、工具返回注入、记忆中毒、记忆提取、歧义驱动的不安全推理）。设定三个独立评估端点：语义攻击接受度、审计可见危害证据、沙箱可观察工具/状态危害。在5个智能体端点和4种提示策略下进行评估。

Result: 不同端点捕捉到不同的失败模式：无额外提示保护下语义失败率在9.0%-44.2%之间；审计可见危害范围小于语义失败；在12,000行匹配分析中，347个沙箱危害中有291个出现在语义检查通过的样本中，表明语义合规不等于实际安全。

Conclusion: SafeClawBench提供了一个可复现的框架，用于在不混淆文本合规性、证据支持危害和可执行状态变化的情况下，比较智能体模型和提示策略的安全效果。

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents introduce security failures that go beyond unsafe text: they can disclose protected objects, write persistent memory, send messages, modify databases, or trigger harmful code and tool effects. Existing evaluations often collapse these stages into a single attack success rate, making it difficult to tell whether a model merely agreed with an attacker or actually produced observable harm. We introduce SafeClawBench, a staged benchmark for tool-using agent security with 600 controlled adversarial tasks across six attack families: direct and indirect prompt injection, tool-return injection, memory poisoning, memory extraction, and ambiguity-driven unsafe inference. SafeClawBench reports three separate endpoints: semantic attack acceptance, audit-visible harm evidence, and sandbox-observed tool/state harm. Evaluating five agent endpoints under four prompt-level policies, we find that these endpoints capture different failure modes. Without additional prompt protection, semantic failure rates vary widely across models, from 9.0% to 44.2%. Audited harm evidence is narrower than semantic failure, and under a separate executable protocol some matched task identities produce sandbox harm despite passing the Semantic Core call: in a 12,000-row matched analysis, 291 of 347 observed sandbox harms occur in rows that pass the semantic check. Prompt policies change endpoint outcomes, but their effects depend on both model and protocol. SafeClawBench provides a reproducible framework for comparing agent models and prompt-policy conditions without conflating textual compliance, evidence-supported harm, and executable state changes. The open-source dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/sairights/safeclawbench.

</details>


### [91] [Evaluating the Effectiveness of LLMs in Aiding Compliance Testing of PKCS#1-v1.5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18405)
*Polina Kozyreva,Endadul Hoque*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了将语法级变异与基于LLM的代码合成相结合的方法，用于二进制协议规范合规性测试，在PKCS#1 v1.5签名验证上复现了10/13个违规类别并发现1个新差异，但LLM幻觉（生成脚本的82.5%）严重限制了有效性，操作可靠性（99.8%）与语义保真度（17.5%）之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制协议实现需要满足结构和语义约束的测试输入，纯随机生成和原始变异难以探索有意义的语义行为，而领域特定合规性测试工具需要深度协议专家知识和大量手工工作。本文旨在探索语法级变异结合LLM代码合成是否可作为更通用的规范合规性测试方法。

Method: 采用语法级变异（structural/constraint mutations）与LLM-based代码合成相结合的方法，在PKCS#1 v1.5签名验证标准上，对48个密码学库实现进行测试。以Morpheus的形式化验证测试预言作为基准。

Result: 成功复现了13个非平凡规范违规类别中的10个，包括全部5个签名伪造类别；发现1个之前未报告的差异。LLM幻觉发生在82.5%的生成脚本中；结构变异的保真度为13.3%，约束变异正确率为30.3%但完全忽略变异率达8.1%；识别出五种不同幻觉类型。

Conclusion: 操作可靠性（99.8%）与语义保真度（17.5%）之间存在巨大差距；LLM幻觉是制约有效性的主要因素，而非变异策略本身。研究为LLM代码合成在规范驱动的测试流水线中的可信应用提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Testing implementations of binary protocols for specification compliance requires inputs that satisfy both structural and semantic constraints. Purely random generation and primitive mutations are often insufficient for exploring semantically meaningful behaviors in protocols that rely on Type-Length-Value (TLV) encoding, yet domain-specific compliance testing tools require deep protocol expertise and significant manual effort to construct. This work investigates whether grammar-level mutation combined with LLM-based code synthesis can serve as a viable, more generalizable approach to specification compliance testing.
  We evaluate the approach on PKCS#1 v1.5 signature verification -- a widely deployed TLV-encoded standard with a formally verified testing oracle (Morpheus) -- across 48 cryptographic library implementations. We reproduced 10 of 13 non-trivial specification violation categories previously identified by Morpheus, including all 5 signature forgery categories, and discovered 1 previously unreported discrepancy. We found that LLM hallucination -- occurring in 82.5% of generated scripts -- is the primary factor limiting effectiveness, not the mutation strategies. We identify five distinct hallucination types and show that their distribution varies systematically across mutation categories: structural mutations are implemented with 13.3% fidelity while constraint mutations achieve 30.3% correctness but suffer the highest rate of mutations being fully ignored (8.1%). These findings reveal a striking gap between operational reliability (99.8%) and semantic fidelity (17.5%), providing actionable guidance on when LLM-based code synthesis can be trusted in specification-driven testing pipelines.

</details>


### [92] [Understanding the "Airport" Censorship Circumvention Ecosystem in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18427)
*Rumaisa Habib,Mingshi Wu,Shiva Shahandeh,Min Ni,Eric Wustrow,Zakir Durumeric*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了中国"机场"（付费翻墙代理）生态系统，通过用户调查、社交媒体分析和网络测量发现，这种工具是中国最流行的翻墙方式，超过一半的受访者使用，但它带来了新的安全风险和审查控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中国地下市场存在大量向用户出售订阅制翻墙代理（即"机场"）的现象，但学术界对这一生态系统的规模、特性和影响缺乏系统性认识。

Method: 综合采用三种方法：用户问卷调查（1,667名受访者）、社交媒体分析（扫描网络并爬取Telegram公告频道）以及主动网络测量（订阅35个机场并测试性能）。

Result: 识别出3,431个活跃机场，发现超过半数受访者使用机场，其性能常优于直连（因采用多跳架构），但存在通过支付宝商业收费、频繁遭遇政府查封、用户难以优化配置等问题，且许多机场实施自身特有的审查策略。

Conclusion: 机场比学术界研究的其他翻墙工具更广泛使用，但引入了新的脆弱性和控制机制，为未来的翻墙研究提供了经验教训和新方向。

Abstract: In China, a burgeoning underground market sells citizens subscription-based censorship circumvention proxies known as ''airports''. We present the first systematic study of this ecosystem, combining user surveys, social media analysis, and active network measurements. We find that airports are by far the most popular off-the-shelf censorship circumvention tool in China, used by over half of our 1,667~survey respondents, who cite their ease of use, performance, and access to geo-restricted services like ChatGPT and Netflix. By scanning the Internet and scraping Telegram announcement channels, we identify 3,431 active airports built on a handful of open-source toolkits. We subscribe to 35 airports and characterize their performance, which often surpasses direct connections through the Great Firewall due to a distinctive multi-hop architecture. However, airports also pose new challenges and security risks: they accept payment through commercial services like Alipay, suffer frequent government takedowns, and are difficult for clients to configure optimally. Many airports also deploy their own distinct censorship policies. Airports are far more widely used than other circumvention tools from the academic literature, but introduce new forms of fragility and control, offering both lessons and opportunities for future circumvention research.

</details>


### [93] [Ghost Vectors: Soft-Deleted Embeddings Remain Reconstructible in HNSW Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18497)
*Chandranil Chakraborttii,Jackeline García Alvarado,Sitora Abdulofizova,Shivanshu Dwivedi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了HNSW向量数据库中软删除操作的隐私漏洞，证明即使标记为删除的向量仍可通过原始索引文件物理恢复，并提出了“Epoch密钥轮换”机制，通过加密向量并在删除时丢弃密钥，将PII恢复率降至0%，同时生成ECDSA签名的可审计删除证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代RAG系统使用HNSW向量数据库进行相似性搜索，但用户请求数据删除时仅执行软删除（标记删除而非物理移除），导致已删除的嵌入向量在磁盘上仍可恢复。这违反了GDPR第17条（被遗忘权）和HIPAA等数据删除与保留合规要求。

Method: （1）在三种HNSW实现上验证删除向量可物理恢复；（2）使用Vec2Text反转模型（无需领域微调）在多种真实数据集上测试恢复效果，包括Wikipedia BLP（人名/地理）、NIH Synthea（患者年龄/性别）、病理组织图像和面部嵌入；（3）提出Epoch密钥轮换：加密向量，删除时丢弃密钥，并生成ECDSA签名的加密删除证明。

Result: Wikipedia BLP：精确人名恢复率25.5%，地理位置恢复率46.4%（ROUGE-L 0.185）。NIH Synthea：患者年龄和性别标记恢复率达100%（ROUGE-L 0.290）。图像嵌入：病理组织分类100%正确（p=1.02e-07），面部嵌入top-1身份恢复99%（p<0.01）。Epoch密钥轮换将PII恢复降至0%，耗时2.5ms/500条（约0.005ms/条）。

Conclusion: HNSW的软删除机制存在严重隐私合规风险，现有RAG系统无法真正满足数据删除要求。Epoch密钥轮换提供了一种高效、可审计的解决方案，能彻底防止已删除向量被恢复。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) allows large language models to access external and private corpora for factual, domain-specific responses. Modern RAG pipelines use hierarchical navigable small world (HNSW) vector databases for efficient similarity search. When a user requests data deletion, the systems typically only mark the record as deleted, leaving the embedding on disk physically unchanged. This soft-delete operation raises compliance concerns under data-erasure and retention requirements such as GDPR Article 17 and HIPAA. Analysis on three HNSW implementations confirms that deleted vectors remain physically recoverable by accessing the raw index files at the storage layer, bypassing API access. Using the Vec2Text inversion model without domain-specific fine-tuning, we show this vulnerability on multiple real-world datasets and data modalities. On Wikipedia biographical living persons dataset (BLP), we successfully recover 25.5% of exact person names and 46.4% of geographic locations (ROUGE-L 0.185). Recovery reaches 100% for both patient age and gender markers (ROUGE-L 0.290) on highly structured, sensitive data (NIH Synthea dataset). On soft-deleted image embeddings, we show 100% tissue classification on histopathology patches (p=1.02e-07) and top-1 identity recovery reaches 99% on facial embeddings (p<0.01). This work introduces Epoch Key Rotation, which encrypts vectors and discards the key upon deletion. Epoch key rotation reduces observed PII recovery to 0% and completes in 2.5 ms for 500 deleted vectors (approximately 0.005 ms/record). Additionally, it generates an ECDSA-signed cryptographic proof as an auditable record of the deletion event.

</details>


### [94] [From Bits to Mixed-Radix Keys: Horner Decomposition, Uniform Sampling, and the Information-Theoretic QKD Interface of the MR-OTP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18526)
*Fabio F. G. Buono*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出混合基数一次性密码本（MR-OTP），将经典OTP扩展到异构字母表同时保持完美保密性。提供了一种无偏方法将QKD源的原始二进制熵转换为均匀混合基数密钥，证明了Horner方法及其逆是二进制整数与混合基数元组之间的自然映射。证明了朴素模约简会导致偏差，而拒绝采样能以最优期望成本恢复均匀性。建立了单会话和多会话管道的端到端信息论安全性，量化了效率提升，提出了批处理提取器，并给出了基础恢复问题的无条件与条件结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典OTP从同质二进制字母表扩展到异构（混合基数）字母表，使一次性密码本能够适配不同长度和字符集的明文，同时保持完美保密性。解决从QKD等二进制熵源生成均匀混合基数密钥的实际无偏转换问题。

Method: 使用Horner方法及其逆作为二进制整数与混合基数元组之间的自然双射映射；分析朴素模约简的偏差问题并采用拒绝采样恢复均匀性；设计批处理提取器以提升效率；建立端到端信息论安全模型。

Result: 证明了拒绝采样以最优期望成本恢复均匀性；量化了MR-OTP相比传统OTP的效率提升；给出了基础恢复问题的无条件与条件安全性结果。

Conclusion: MR-OTP在保持信息论安全性的前提下，有效支持异构字母表场景，提供了从二进制熵源到混合基数密钥的无偏转换方案，具有理论完备性和实际可行性。

Abstract: The Mixed-Radix One-Time Pad (MR-OTP) extends the classical OTP to heterogeneous alphabets while preserving perfect secrecy. We provide a practical, bias-free method to convert raw binary entropy from a QKD source into uniform mixed-radix keys by identifying Horner's method and its inverse as the natural mapping between binary integers and mixed-radix tuples. We show that naive modular reduction induces bias and prove that rejection sampling restores uniformity with optimal expected cost. We establish end-to-end information-theoretic security for single and multi-session pipelines, quantify efficiency gains, present a batched extractor, and give unconditional and conditional results on the Base Recovery Problem.

</details>


### [95] [Evaluating Prompting-Based Defenses Against Domain-Camouflaged Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18530)
*Aaditya Pai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五种基于提示的防御策略（重点标注、改写、提示夹层及两种组合）对抗领域伪装注入攻击的效果，实验涵盖三个模型族（Claude Haiku、Llama 3.1 8B、Gemini 2.0 Flash）和三个应用领域（金融、法律、通用），共3,510次试验。结果表明，改写检索内容是最稳定的防御策略，可将攻击成功率降低55-84%；防御效果高度依赖模型；金融领域面临最高残余风险（基线攻击成功率26-33%），且无提示防御能在弱模型上完全消除威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域伪装注入攻击使用领域专业词汇将恶意指令嵌入检索内容中，能够绕过依赖句法注入标记的传统检测器。当检测失败时，实践者需要了解哪些防御架构能有效降低攻击成功率。目前缺乏针对此类伪装类注入攻击的提示防御系统的评估。

Method: 在三个模型族（Claude Haiku、Llama 3.1 8B、Gemini 2.0 Flash）和三个部署领域（金融、法律、通用）上，通过3,510次试验系统评估五种提示防御策略：重点标注、改写、提示夹层及两种组合方案，并与Llama Guard 4基线对比。

Result: 改写检索内容是最一致的防御策略，攻击成功率降低55-84%；防御效果高度模型依赖（重点标注在Claude Haiku上减半攻击成功率，但对Llama 3.1 8B无效）；金融领域基线攻击成功率26-33%，残余风险最高；无提示防御能在弱模型上完全消除威胁。

Conclusion: 提供了首个针对伪装类注入攻击的提示防御系统评估，建立了基于基准的实践建议。但所有任务使用合成构建的专业文档，基准排名是否能泛化到真实企业文档仍有待验证。

Abstract: Domain-camouflaged injection attacks embed malicious instructions in retrieved content using domain-appropriate vocabulary, evading standard detectors that rely on syntactic injection markers. When detection fails, practitioners need to know which defense architectures reduce attack success. We evaluate five prompting-based defenses (spotlighting, paraphrasing, prompt sandwiching, and two combinations) against domain-camouflaged injection across three model families (Claude Haiku, Llama 3.1 8B, Gemini 2.0 Flash) and three deployment domains (financial, legal, general) using 3,510 trials. Paraphrasing retrieved content before agent processing is the most consistently effective defense in this benchmark, reducing camouflage attack success rate by 55-84\% depending on model, and achieves lower attack success rates than our Llama Guard 4 configuration on every model tested. Defense effectiveness is strongly model-dependent: spotlighting halves attack success on Claude Haiku but provides no benefit on Llama 3.1 8B. Financial domain deployments face the highest residual risk at 26-33\% baseline attack success rate, with no prompting-based defense fully eliminating the threat on weaker models. These results provide the first systematic evaluation of prompting-based defenses specifically against camouflage-class injection attacks and establish benchmark-based recommendations for practitioners. All tasks use synthetically constructed professional documents; whether these benchmark rankings generalize to real enterprise documents remains an open question.

</details>


### [96] [AI Sandboxes: A Threat Model, Taxonomy, and Measurement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18532)
*Inderjeet Singh,Haitham Mahmoud,Andrés Murillo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地提出了AI沙箱的保障性分析框架，形式化了沙箱边界与最弱链路组合规则，定义了数字AI、具身自主与信息物理系统场景下的沙箱原型、威胁模型与测量框架，并通过三个案例验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在物理世界、AIoT和信息物理系统中的部署日益增多，现有沙箱概念缺乏统一的形式化定义和保障性评估框架，难以支撑安全、监管与可信部署的测试需求。

Method: 首先形式化沙箱边界及基于"最弱链路"的证据组合规则；分类提出主要沙箱原型；构建涵盖物理过程与保障机制攻击的信息物理威胁模型；设计包含保真度、可控性、可观测性、约束性、可复现性和治理工件的测量框架，并在三个真实沙箱案例上实施评估。

Result: 提出了一套完整的威胁模型、分类体系与测量框架，明确了不同沙箱能够有效测试的内容、可管控的风险类型以及能支撑的安全、安保与监管保障证据形式。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI沙箱的设计、评估与认证提供了理论依据和实用工具，有助于弥合数字AI、具身自主与信息物理系统之间在测试与保障方面的概念鸿沟。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly evaluated in bounded environments that combine isolation, simulation, instrumentation, supervision, and evidence capture. For physical AI, AIoT, and cyber-physical systems, this shift is not a matter of terminology: the system under test may sense, decide, actuate, communicate, and fail through physical processes, networked devices, and human operators. This article develops an assurance-oriented account of AI sandboxes as controlled environments for testing, evaluation, verification, and validation across digital AI, embodied autonomy, and cyber-physical deployments. We formalize the sandbox boundary and a weakest-link rule for composing per-dimension evidence into a bounded deployment claim; separate major sandbox archetypes; define a cyber-physical threat model that includes attacks on the assurance apparatus itself; and introduce a measurement framework spanning fidelity, controllability, observability, containment, reproducibility, and governance artifacts, instantiated on three worked case studies of real sandboxes. The resulting threat model, taxonomy, and measurement framework clarify what a sandbox can validly test, which risks it can contain, and what forms of evidence it can support for safety, security, and regulatory assurance.

</details>


### [97] [Confident yet Concerned: Inconsistencies in Computing Students' Attitudes on Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18541)
*Victor Adama,Robert Biddle,Nalin Arachchilage,Danielle Lottridge*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对236名计算机专业大学生的调查，研究发现这些精通技术的年轻人在网络安全方面表现出合理的意识，但存在实践不一致、错误认知持续存在、课堂外学习更有效、遭遇网络犯罪但支持有限等矛盾，并通过聚类分析识别出两个态度群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前年轻成年人深陷技术环境，在管理在线隐私方面普遍感到无力，且特别容易受到网络钓鱼攻击，这引发了他们对待和管理网络安全的总体态度问题。研究旨在更好地理解精通技术的年轻成年人如何看待网络安全，特别是他们应对欺骗性在线通信的知识、态度和经验。

Method: 对236名计算机专业大学生（作为"领先用户"）进行问卷调查，结合开放式回答的主题分析与定量数据，并通过聚类分析识别态度群体。

Result: 学生表现出合理的网络安全意识，但经验各异，存在实践、责任认知和支持结构方面的不一致。揭示四个关键主题张力：1) 知识丰富但存在持续错误信念；2) 更多从课堂外学习安全知识；3) 援助有限且曾成为网络犯罪受害者；4) 部分自信但部分担忧自身安全和责任。聚类分析识别出两个群体，一组感觉准备不足、信心较低但渴望学习。意图和客观知识的测量与准备度相关，自我效能感与信心相关并能预测聚类归属。

Conclusion: 即使是技术精通的年轻成年人，在网络安全方面也存在明显的知识-实践差距和支持缺口，不同态度群体有不同的教育和支持需求，自我效能感是区分群体的关键因素。

Abstract: Today's young adults are most immersed in technology, leading in feelings of powerlessness in managing online privacy across many platforms, and particularly susceptible to phishing attacks. This raises questions about their general, wide-ranging attitudes towards and management of cybersecurity. How do young, tech-savvy adults approach cybersecurity? We seek a better understanding of their cybersecurity knowledge, attitudes and experiences, in particular in addressing deceptive online communications. We surveyed a group of `lead users': computing university students (n = 236). By combining thematic analysis of open-ended responses with quantitative data, we provide insights into their experiences and perceptions. While students demonstrate reasonable cybersecurity awareness, their cybersecurity experiences vary, and inconsistencies exist around their practices, perceptions of responsibility, and support structures. Findings also reveal four key thematic tensions: 1) Computing students are knowledgeable yet have persistent incorrect beliefs, 2) They learn more about keeping safe from sources outside the classroom, 3) They have limited assistance and have fallen victim to cybercrime, and 4) Many are confident, yet others are concerned about their own safety and responsibility. Through cluster analysis of attitudes, we identify two groups, with one feeling less prepared, less confident, yet expressing a desire to learn more. Established measures of intentions and objective knowledge were correlated to preparedness. Self-efficacy correlated to confidence and predicted cluster membership.

</details>


### [98] [The Gate Is Only as Honest as Its Contracts: ContractGuard for the Contract Layer of Risk-Aware Causal Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18550)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer,Rahul Suresh Babu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出风险感知因果门控（RACG）防御工具增强型LLM代理免受间接提示注入攻击，通过从代理可见动作空间中移除危险工具实现结构性保障。关键发现：结构性保障并未消除信任假设，而是将其转移到工具合约（前置条件、效果、风险、授权）的完整性上；伪造工具效果比篡改风险标签更危险，因为因果门控在准入门控之前生效。作者提出ContractGuard验证器，在注册表和门控之间添加签名来源、类型化合约认证和运行时效果验证，在受控基准测试中将注入成功率降至零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工具增强型LLM代理容易受到间接提示注入攻击，攻击者可以通过操纵代理行为来调用危险工具。作者旨在设计一种具有结构性安全保障的防御机制，并揭示其中仍存在的信任假设及其薄弱环节。

Method: 提出风险感知因果门控（RACG）机制，通过从代理可见动作空间中移除危险工具来防御注入攻击。进一步提出ContractGuard验证器，在工具注册表和门控之间增加三层验证：签名来源、类型化合约认证和运行时效果验证。

Result: 在受控基准测试中，ContractGuard在针对所有建模攻击（包括穷举白盒自适应攻击者）的情况下，将注入成功率恢复至零，且不会过度拒绝诚实合约。在六个当前代托管模型上验证了结构性预测。

Conclusion: 结构性保障本身不足以消除信任假设；工具合约完整性（尤其是效果完整性而非风险标签）是承载信任的关键。ContractGuard通过多层验证有效地解决了这一信任问题。

Abstract: Risk-Aware Causal Gating (RACG) defends tool-augmented LLM agents against indirect prompt injection by removing dangerous tools from the agent's visible action space, so that even a fully injection-compliant agent cannot call a tool it cannot see. We make three points. First, this structural guarantee does not eliminate the trust assumption behind safe tool use; it relocates it into the integrity of the tool contracts -- declared preconditions, effects, risk, and authorization -- that the gate reads, so an attacker who corrupts a contract can make the gate mis-decide without ever persuading the agent. Second, forging a tool's effects is strictly more dangerous than tampering with its risk label, because RACG applies a causal gate before its admissibility gate: an off-path tool is never exposed, so risk-relabeling alone fails, whereas effect forgery routes the dangerous tool onto the causal path and succeeds. Effect integrity, not the risk label, is the load-bearing assumption. Third, we introduce ContractGuard, a verifier between the registry and the gate that layers signed provenance, typed contract attestation, and runtime effect verification; on a controlled benchmark it restores injection success to zero against every modeled attack -- including an exhaustive white-box adaptive attacker -- without over-rejecting honest contracts, and the structural prediction is confirmed on six current-generation hosted models (Claude Opus 4.8, Sonnet 4.6, Haiku 4.5; Amazon Nova Premier and Nova 2 Lite; GPT-OSS-120B).

</details>


### [99] [MIDS: Detecting Stealthy Masquerade and Tampering Attacks on CAN Bus via Bidirectional Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18599)
*Qiqi Liu,Runhan Song,Lei Cui,Heng Zhang,Yuyan Sun,Limin Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Mamba入侵检测系统(MIDS)，采用双流双向选择状态空间模型并行处理CAN标识符和负载，以检测传统方法难以识别的伪装攻击。在特斯拉Model 3采集的超过1亿帧数据和54种伪装攻击变体上，MIDS的F1达96.94%，超过最强基线8个百分点；在四个公开基准上F1达93.70%-99.61%，推理延迟仅1.147ms，适用于实时车载部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAN协议缺乏加密和认证机制，存在严重安全威胁。现有入侵检测系统主要针对注入式攻击（DoS、模糊测试、ID欺骗），利用帧间到达时间等统计特征进行检测；但面对更难的伪装攻击场景（内部攻击者在原始传输时隙替换合法帧，保持流量周期性），传统基于流量统计的防御方法失效，亟需新的检测方案。

Method: 提出MIDS双流框架，并行处理CAN标识符和负载数据，通过双向选择性状态空间模型重建二者的联合时序语义。收集了来自物理特斯拉Model 3在三种驾驶模式下超过1亿帧CAN数据，合成54种伪装攻击变体（包括仅ID修改、仅数据修改和两者组合修改），并在四个公开基准上验证泛化能力。

Result: MIDS在特斯拉数据集上F1达96.94%，超过最强可重现基线8个百分点以上；单窗口推理延迟1.147ms，满足实时部署需求。在ROAD、CrySyS、OTIDS、CT&T四个公开基准上，F1达93.70%-99.61%，在统一的5折交叉验证协议下超过八个基线最高达13.94个百分点。

Conclusion: MIDS有效解决了伪装攻击检测难题，其双流双向状态空间建模方法在保持低延迟的前提下显著提升了检测性能，并在多种攻击场景和数据集上展现出良好的泛化能力，具备实时车载部署的可行前景。

Abstract: The Controller Area Network (CAN) protocol is the primary communication standard for Electronic Control Units (ECUs) in modern vehicles, but its lack of encryption and authentication exposes it to a range of security threats. Existing intrusion detection systems are largely tuned to fabrication-style attacks (DoS, fuzzing, ID spoofing realised by frame injection), in which detection signals such as per-ID inter-arrival statistics are readily available. We instead address the harder \emph{masquerade} setting~\cite{b37}, in which an internal adversary substitutes a legitimate frame in-situ at its original transmission slot, preserving traffic periodicity and rendering traffic-statistic defences ineffective. We propose the Mamba Intrusion Detection System (MIDS), an innovative dual-stream framework that processes CAN identifiers and payloads in parallel and reconstructs their joint temporal semantics through bidirectional selective state-space modelling. To evaluate MIDS, we collected over 100 million CAN frames from a physical Tesla Model 3 across three driving regimes and synthesised 54 masquerade attack variants spanning ID-only, data-only, and combined modifications. MIDS attains an F1 of 96.94\% on this dataset, exceeding the strongest reproducible baseline by more than 8 percentage points, while sustaining a 1.147~ms single-window inference latency -- ample headroom for real-time onboard deployment. To verify generalisation, we further evaluate MIDS on four public benchmarks (ROAD, CrySyS, OTIDS, CT\&T) covering both masquerade and injection scenarios; MIDS attains F1 from 93.70\% to 99.61\%, outperforming the strongest of eight reproduced baselines by up to 13.94 percentage points under a unified 5-fold protocol.

</details>


### [100] [Code-Augur: Agentic Vulnerability Detection via Specification Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18619)
*Zhengxiong Luo,Mehtab Zafar,Dylan Wolff,Abhik Roychoudhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种"安全规范优先"的范式，通过将LLM代理的隐式假设显式化为安全规范，并利用运行时证伪持续优化这些规范。实现为Code-Augur系统，在真实项目中发现了22个新漏洞，比现有最先进代理检测出更多漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主LLM代理进行漏洞检测时，其推理过程不透明、假设未经验证，导致漏报和信任缺失。代理可能基于错误的假设判断函数是安全的，从而遗漏真实漏洞。

Method: (1) 安全规范优先范式：将代理关于组件安全的隐式假设显式化为源码级断言；(2) 运行时证伪循环：使用引导式模糊测试器尝试触发这些断言，若被触发则发现真实漏洞或修正有缺陷的规范；(3) 实现为Code-Augur系统，基于通用LLM（Sonnet、DeepSeek）构建。

Result: 在真实项目中，Code-Augur比现有最先进代理检测出更多漏洞；在关键开源项目中发现了22个新漏洞；基于通用LLM（Sonnet、DeepSeek）即可达到甚至超越专用模型（Claude Mythos）的效果。

Conclusion: 通过显式化并验证代理的安全假设，可以有效提升自动化漏洞检测的可信度与效果。Code-Augur展示了利用通用LLM进行高效代理式漏洞检测的可行性。

Abstract: The advent of agentic vulnerability detection is already becoming a watershed moment for software security. Audits conducted entirely by autonomous LLM agents are uncovering critical vulnerabilities in fundamental software underpinning digital society. Many of these vulnerabilities remained masked for years, surfacing only now with AI agents. Yet the reasoning behind these discoveries remains alarmingly opaque and unvalidated. What assumptions did the agent make about a function's inputs when it deemed that function to be secure? Failures in reasoning and incorrect assumptions can lead to missed vulnerabilities and reduce trust in agentic analysis.
  We propose a security-specification-first paradigm that (1) exposes the agent's tacit assumptions explicitly as security specifications and (2) continuously refines those specifications via runtime falsification. We realize our approach in Code-Augur, a novel harness for agentic vulnerability detection. Given a codebase, Code-Augur analyzes each component of the system for vulnerable code. When it deems a component to be secure, it commits the local invariants behind that judgment as in-source assertions. In parallel, Code-Augur leverages a guided fuzzer to attempt to falsify those assumptions. When the fuzzer triggers an assertion, this either reveals a genuine vulnerability or a flawed specification to refine. In both cases, this process grounds the agent's understanding, aligning its view of code intent with how the code actually behaves. On real-world subjects, Code-Augur effectively leverages security specifications to detect more vulnerabilities than other state-of-the-art agents. Additionally, Code-Augur found 22 new vulnerabilities in key open-source projects. Compared to curated specialized models like Claude Mythos, Code-Augur offers effective agentic vulnerability detection built on widely available LLMs like Sonnet and DeepSeek.

</details>


### [101] [TGCM: Topic-Guided Generative Disentanglement of Interleaved APT Technique Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18651)
*Guo-Wei Wong,Ming-Chuan Yang,Shou-De Lin,Wang-Chien Lee,Meng~Chang Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Topic-Guided Consistency Modeling (TGCM)框架，用于解决企业环境中多APT攻击序列交织的未知数量序列分离问题。TGCM利用一致性模型进行单步逆映射，结合MITRE ATT&CK主题先验实现语义一致的攻击链分解，在合成数据集、混合数据集及DARPA TC-E3/E5真实追踪上超越15种基线方法，并在零样本场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中多个APT攻击并发进行，产生的审计日志中不同攻击源的技术序列随时间交织，现有方法（统计模式挖掘、溯源分析）大多假设单一攻击场景或依赖刚性启发式规则，无法有效处理攻击重叠、技术共享和变长执行等现实复杂情况。

Method: 提出TGCM生成式解纠缠框架：1) 利用一致性模型学习从交织多源观察到结构化单源序列的直接逆映射，单步推理即可完成分解；2) 引入基于MITRE ATT&CK叙事推导的主题引导先验，提供高层战术约束以保持攻击链的语义连贯性。

Result: 在合成数据集、混合数据集及DARPA TC-E3和TC-E5真实攻击追踪上评估，与15种基线方法（模式挖掘、深度学习、LLM方法）对比，在高交织和技术共享条件下分离鲁棒性显著提升，且在无需重新训练的情况下零样本泛化至DARPA TC-E5自然交织基准。

Conclusion: TGCM有效解决了未知数量交织攻击序列的分离问题，通过生成式建模与领域知识引导相结合的方式，在复杂真实场景下展现出优越的分离性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: In enterprise environments, multiple Advanced Persistent Threat (APT) campaigns often unfold concurrently, producing audit logs in which attack techniques across actors (sources) are interleaved over time. This setting naturally gives rise to an Unknown-K Interleaved Sequence Demixing (UKISD) problem: recovering multiple latent campaigns from an interleaved technique sequence while jointly inferring their number and technique-level assignments. Existing approaches, ranging from statistical pattern mining to provenance-based analysis, typically assume single-campaign settings or rely on rigid heuristics, limiting their effectiveness under realistic conditions involving overlapping campaigns, shared techniques, and variable execution lengths.
  We present Topic-Guided Consistency Modeling (TGCM), a generative disentanglement framework to tackle the UKSID problem. TGCM leverages Consistency Models to learn a direct inverse mapping from interleaved multi-campaign observations to structured single-campaign sequences in a single inference step. To favor semantically coherent attack chains, TGCM incorporates a topic-guided prior derived from MITRE ATT\&CK narratives, providing high-level tactical constraints during decomposition. We evaluate TGCM on synthetic datasets, established mixed datasets, and incident traces from DARPA TC-E3 and TC-E5, comparing against 15 representative baselines spanning pattern mining, deep learning, and LLM-based methods. Results indicate improved separation robustness over baselines under heavy interleaving and technique sharing, and show that TGCM generalizes zero-shot to a naturally interleaved in-the-wild benchmark (DARPA TC-E5) without retraining.

</details>


### [102] [Understanding and Mitigating Prompt Leaking Attacks in Real-World LLM-Based Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18673)
*Yong Yang,Chong Fu,Tong Zhang,Rui Zeng,Qingming Li,Tianyu Du,Zonghui Wang,Shouling Ji,Wenzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对1200个真实世界LLM应用进行系统测量，发现超过80%的应用存在系统提示泄露问题，揭示了注意力漂移机制，并提出了AREA防御方法，在保持可用性的同时有效防御提示泄露攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示作为LLM应用的核心知识产权，容易被泄露攻击窃取。尽管已有相关工作，但真实部署场景下的提示泄露普遍性、成因及防御效果仍不清楚。

Method: 本文首先对六大商业平台的1200个应用进行大规模测量。然后通过注意力层级的机制分析，识别出注意力漂移（包括查询-键对齐偏差和softmax放大）是导致防御失效的根本原因。基于此发现，提出AREA防御方法，通过可优化的软提示重新锚定模型注意力。

Result: 超过80%的部署在对抗性查询下泄露系统提示；现有防御在防止泄露的同时往往降低可用性；AREA在匹配最优防御的防泄露能力的同时，将可用性提升33%以上，优化开销降低近3倍；负责任披露使两个厂商将此类漏洞分类为中危。

Conclusion: 系统提示泄露在真实LLM应用中普遍存在且危害严重，现有防御效果不佳。本文提出的AREA方法基于注意力漂移机制的洞察，实现了防泄露与可用性的更好平衡，为该安全领域提供了可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based applications rely on system prompts to encode core logic and developer-defined constraints, making these prompts important intellectual property. However, system prompts are vulnerable to prompt leaking attacks. Although prior work has shown such attacks in controlled settings, their prevalence, causes, and defenses in real-world deployments remain unclear.
  This paper presents a systematic study of prompt leaking in real-world LLM-based applications. We measure 1,200 applications across six major commercial platforms and find that over 80% of deployments leak system prompts under realistic adversarial queries, sometimes exposing sensitive information such as third-party API keys. We also show that existing defenses often fail to prevent leakage without degrading usability. To explain these failures, we conduct an attention-level mechanistic analysis and identify attention drift, where query-key alignment bias and softmax amplification cause LLMs to progressively ignore defensive constraints. Guided by this insight, we propose AREA, a practical defense that re-anchors the model's attention using an optimizable soft prompt. Experiments and real-world case studies show that AREA matches the leakage resistance of state-of-the-art defenses while improving average usability by over 33% and reducing optimization overhead by nearly 3x. Our responsible disclosure led two affected vendors to classify these leaks as medium-severity vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [103] [Image Prompt Reconstruction Attacks on Distributed MLLM Inference Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18710)
*Xinjian Luo,Hongyan Chang,Jianxin Wei,Yuncheng Wu,Xiaofeng Gao,Meikang Qiu,Ting Yu,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次研究分布式多模态大模型（MLLM）推理框架中由中间嵌入引发的图像隐私泄露问题，设计了图像嵌入提取算法，并提出了两种被动黑盒图像重建攻击（MPAA和IEDA），在Gemma 3、Phi 4 Multimodal、Qwen 2.5 VL和Llama 4 Scout上验证了攻击有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式LLM推理框架中中间嵌入会泄露文本prompt，随着LLM演进为多模态大模型（MLLM），图像prompt包含的丰富视觉和语义信息使其中间嵌入具有高度隐私敏感性，但MLLM中图像prompt的泄露风险尚未被充分探索。

Method: 首先分析图像像素到中间表示的信息流，设计图像嵌入提取算法实现100%提取准确率；在此基础上提出两种被动黑盒攻击：MPAA通过逐块信息提取与组装实现细粒度像素级重建，IEDA通过嵌入引导的扩散生成实现粗粒度语义重建。

Result: 在Gemma 3、Phi 4 Multimodal、Qwen 2.5 VL和Llama 4 Scout上取得了一致优越的重建性能，并分析了MoE架构、图像预处理、模型大小和文本-图像依赖关系对攻击性能的影响。

Conclusion: 这是首个关于MLLM图像重建攻击的研究，揭示了分布式MLLM推理中图像隐私泄露的严重风险。

Abstract: Distributed large language model (LLM) inference frameworks connect isolated consumer-grade devices for large-scale model inference, substantially reducing hardware constraints. However, recent studies show that intermediate embeddings transmitted among participants can leak private prompts. As LLMs evolve into multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), this risk extends beyond text: image prompts contain rich visual and semantic information, making their intermediate embeddings highly privacy-sensitive. Yet, image-prompt leakage in distributed MLLM inference remains largely unexplored.
  In this paper, we investigate privacy risks to input images caused by intermediate embeddings in distributed MLLM frameworks. We first analyze the information flow from image pixels to intermediate representations. Since image and text embeddings are often intertwined across MLLM layers, we design an image embedding extraction algorithm as a prerequisite for reconstruction attacks, achieving 100% extraction accuracy across almost all MLLM layers in our experiments. Building on this, we develop two passive black-box image reconstruction attacks, MPAA and IEDA, reflecting realistic threats from normal participants with limited knowledge and capability. MPAA performs fine-grained pixel-level reconstruction via patch-wise information extraction and assembly, while IEDA performs coarse-grained semantic reconstruction through embedding-guided diffusion generation.
  We evaluate our attacks on four representative MLLM families: Gemma 3, Phi 4 Multimodal, Qwen 2.5 VL, and Llama 4 Scout. Results show consistently superior reconstruction performance in various settings. We further analyze the effects of MoE architecture, image preprocessing, model size, and text-image dependency on attack performance. To our knowledge, this is the first study of image reconstruction attacks on MLLMs.

</details>


### [104] [A Predictive Neural Network Architecture for Early Detection of Low-Rate Cyberattacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18771)
*Mert Nakıp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出IDQS框架，通过RTP-QoS神经网络预测QoS趋势并结合PDM决策模型检测预测与实际QoS的偏差，在IoT网络中实现低速率拒绝服务攻击的早期检测，检测准确率超过79%和91%，推理时间仅0.28秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDoS攻击因其隐蔽性和持续性难以被传统入侵检测系统识别，对IoT网络构成严重威胁，亟需轻量级、高效的早期检测方案。

Method: IDQS框架包含两个核心组件：(i) RTP-QoS，一种循环趋势预测神经网络，基于历史流量模式学习和预测未来QoS；(ii) PDM，成对决策模型，通过评估预测QoS与实际QoS之间的差异来识别潜在攻击。

Result: 在SDN-SlowRate-DDoS和CIC-IDS2017数据集上，IDQS分别达到超过79%和91%的检测准确率，具有高召回率和低假阴性率，端到端推理时间仅0.28秒。

Conclusion: IDQS在资源受限的IoT环境中实现了实时部署的有效性和高效性，能够早期检测LDoS攻击。

Abstract: Low-Rate Denial of Service (LDoS) attacks pose a significant challenge to IoT networks due to their subtle and prolonged nature, often evading traditional intrusion detection systems. This paper presents IDQS (Intrusion Detection via QoS Prediction), a lightweight and proactive framework for early LDoS attack detection. IDQS integrates two new key components: (i) RTP-QoS, a Recurrent Trend Predictive Neural Network that learns and forecasts future Quality of Service (QoS) based on historical traffic patterns, and (ii) PDM, a Pairwise Decision Model that evaluates discrepancies between predicted and actual QoS to identify potential attacks. Evaluated on the public SDN-SlowRate-DDoS and CIC-IDS2017 datasets, IDQS respectively achieves over 79% and 91% detection accuracy across most attack scenarios with high recall and low false negatives, while maintaining an end-to-end inference time of just 0.28 seconds. The results demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of IDQS for real-time deployment in resource-constrained IoT environments.

</details>


### [105] [TRAP: Benchmark for Task-completion and Resistance to Active Privacy-extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18996)
*Moon Ye-Bin,Nam Hyeon-Woo,Baek Seong-Eun,Yejin Yeo,Tae-Hyun Oh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TRAP框架评估AI代理在文档密集型任务中隐私保护与任务准确性的权衡。研究发现所有模型都存在非平凡的信息泄露，提示防御会显著降低任务准确率，且理论上软约束防御无法同时实现高任务成功率和零泄露概率。作者提出结构化的私密字段隔离方法（用哈希键替换私密信息），能有效防止泄露同时保持任务准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理在处理包含敏感私密信息的文档时，必须在利用私密信息完成任务和防止信息泄露之间取得平衡。现有模型具备足够能力使用私密信息完成任务，但这种能力也可能被诱导去泄露信息，形成了根本性的矛盾。

Method: 1. 提出TRAP（Task-completion and Resistance to Active Privacy-extraction）评估框架，每个场景包含含私密信息的文档、需要调用正确工具的任务查询、以及试图提取私密信息的攻击查询。2. 评估22个模型（前沿专有和开源模型）。3. 理论证明：对于任何基于softmax的模型，软约束防御无法同时实现高任务成功率和零泄露概率。4. 提出结构化私密字段隔离方法。

Result: 所有模型家族都表现出非平凡的信息泄露；指令遵循能力与泄露率相关；现有提示防御能减少泄露但显著降低任务准确性；提示优化无法摆脱这种权衡；理论证明软约束防御存在不可能性。

Conclusion: 软约束防御（如提示防御）本质上无法同时保证高任务准确率和零泄露概率，因此需要结构性方法——在私密字段到达模型前用哈希键替换，从而根本上阻止泄露。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly deployed in document-intensive workflows where sensitive private information is not an edge case but a routine input, e.g., an agent booking a flight needs passport numbers. In such settings, the agent must use private information to complete tasks accurately while never exposing it in its responses, because it cannot verify who is actually at the keyboard. These two obligations are in fundamental tension. A model capable enough to use private information for task completion can, by the same capability, be induced to reveal it. To evaluate the trade-off of task accuracy and privacy leakage, we introduce Task-completion and Resistance to Active Privacy-extraction (TRAP). Each scenario includes a document containing private information, a task query that requires the agent to invoke the correct tool using private fields, and an attack query that attempts to elicit the same information in natural language. Evaluating 22 models spanning frontier proprietary and open-source models at multiple scales, we find that all model families exhibit non-trivial leakage, and that instruction-following ability correlates with leakage rate. Existing prompt-based defenses reduce leakage but at significant cost to task accuracy. Prompt optimization fails to escape this trade-off. We demonstrate that this failure is not incidental. For any softmax-based model, no soft-constraint defense, e.g., prompt-based defenses, can jointly achieve high task success with zero leakage probability. Motivated by this impossibility result, we propose structural private field isolation, which replaces private fields with hash keys before they reach the model. This approach largely prevents leakage while keeping task accuracy.

</details>


### [106] [Lifecycle-Aware Dynamic Analysis for Secure ML Model Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19023)
*Gabriele Digregorio,Marco Di Gennaro,Francesco Pastore,Stefano Zanero,Stefano Longari,Michele Carminati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Moat，一种动态生命周期感知的ML模型执行安全方法，通过观察模型在生命周期各阶段与主机系统交互的结构化模式来检测恶意行为。其参考实现Re-Moat在77,974个Hugging Face模型、31个CVE利用和334个攻击模型上评估，在保持接近零误报率的同时检测所有攻击类别，优于现有基于静态规则和签名的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ML模型扫描方案主要依赖静态的、格式特定的规则或已知攻击签名，无法跨框架泛化且难以检测新型攻击路径。需要一种更通用、动态的方法来检测嵌入在模型工件中的恶意行为。

Method: 基于两个核心观察：ML模型在定义良好的生命周期阶段内运行，且每个阶段中与主机系统的交互具有高度结构化和可预测性。将这些直觉转化为Moat——一种动态生命周期感知的ML模型执行安全方法，并实现Re-Moat参考系统。

Result: 在77,974个Hugging Face模型、31个CVE PoC和334个攻击模型上，Re-Moat检测所有评估的攻击类别，误报率接近零，优于现有最先进的模型扫描方案。

Conclusion: 验证了动态分析对于保障ML模型执行安全的有效性，表明基于生命周期感知的动态分析是检测恶意模型工件的有力方向。

Abstract: The growing reliance on pre-trained Machine Learning (ML) models has introduced new attack surfaces. Recent vulnerabilities demonstrate that malicious behavior can be embedded within model artifacts, often bypassing existing defenses. Current model-scanning solutions primarily rely on static, format-specific rules or known attack signatures, which limit their ability to generalize across frameworks and to detect novel exploitation paths. In contrast, we propose a solution that focuses on the effects an attack has on the host system executing the model and builds on foundational intuitions about ML model execution. In particular, we observe that ML models operate within well-defined lifecycle phases and that, within each phase, interactions with the host system are highly structured and predictable. We translate these intuitions into Moat, a dynamic lifecycle-aware approach for securing ML model execution, and instantiate this design in Re-Moat, our reference implementation. We evaluate Re-Moat across multiple ML frameworks using 77,974 real-world model artifacts from the Hugging Face Hub, 31 Proofs-of-Concept (PoCs) from CVEs, and 334 models from a state-of-the-art dataset, and compare it against state-of-the-art model-scanning solutions. Our results show that our approach detects all evaluated attack classes while maintaining a close-to-zero false-positive rate, validating our intuitions and motivating dynamic analysis for securing ML model execution.

</details>


### [107] [PYPILINE: Malicious PyPI Package Detection via Suspicious API Knowledge and Agent Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19063)
*Siyuan Pang,Zhengwei Jiang,Yepeng Yao,Zijing Fan,Haozhe Li,Baoxu Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PYPILINE方法，结合可疑API知识库与Agent工作流检测恶意PyPI包，在精确率96.7%、召回率99.6%、F1-score 98.1%上显著优于现有工具，并提供可解释的恶意性评估报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则或传统机器学习的恶意PyPI包检测方法存在可解释性差且难以适应新型攻击的问题，亟需更高效、可解释的检测方案以维护开源软件供应链安全。

Method: 先对已知恶意包进行静态分析，提取抽象语法树并生成API调用图，自动构建结构化可疑API知识库；检测阶段通过Agent工作流结合知识库增强推理能力，对未知包进行深度语义分析，输出结构化可解释的恶意性评估报告。

Result: PYPILINE在精确率96.7%、召回率99.6%、F1-score 98.1%上显著优于现有最优工具，精确率超出基线5.7-24.2个百分点；通过实证研究揭示了常见攻击策略和滥用API。

Conclusion: PYPILINE通过结合可疑API知识库与AI Agent工作流，提供了一种实用、高效、便捷且可解释的恶意包检测方案，有效增强了开源生态系统安全性。

Abstract: The detection of malicious PyPI packages is crucial for maintaining the security of the open source software supply chain. Existing methods, which primarily rely on rules or traditional machine learning, suffer from poor interpretability and difficulty in adapting to novel attacks. To address this, we propose PYPILINE, a novel detection method that combines a suspicious API knowledge base with an Agent workflow. PYPILINE first conducts static analysis on known malicious packages, extracting abstract syntax trees and generating API call graphs, from which it automatically extracts and constructs a structured suspicious API knowledge base. During the detection phase, this knowledge base is used to enhance reasoning capabilities. Through an Agent workflow, PYPILINE performs in depth semantic analysis of unknown packages and outputs a structured, interpretable maliciousness assessment report. The experimental results show that PYPILINE significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art tools in precision of 96.7\%, recall of 99.6\%, and F1-score of 98.1\%, with its precision surpassing baseline tools by 5.7 to 24.2 percentage points. Additionally, we conducted an empirical study on malicious packages, systematically revealing prevalent attack strategies, as well as the most commonly abused APIs. Equipped with tool-calling AI agent workflows for automated vector database retrieval of suspicious API knowledge and mail server delivery of analysis reports, PYPILINE delivers a practical, efficient, and convenient malicious package detection solution to strengthen open-source ecosystem security.

</details>


### [108] [Compute-Budgeted Exploitability Evidence Graphs for Prospective Vulnerability Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19076)
*Faruk Alpay,Taylan Alpay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种泄漏安全的漏洞可利用性预测评估协议，通过在时间证据图上进行预算约束的证据选择，实现了前瞻性漏洞分类。实验表明，每个CVE只需2份证据文档即可捕获大部分价值，且朴素随机分割会高估召回率8.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御者无法同时修补所有新披露的漏洞，因此可利用性预测必须以前瞻性而非回顾性方式进行评估。现有方法常因时间泄漏和信息泄漏而高估预测性能。

Method: 将公告、漏洞档案、修复提交和黑客社区讨论构建为时间证据图；使用预算约束的选择器为每个CVE选取少量证据文档；每个分数附带可审计的证书，包含支持信号、时间戳、源层和泄漏标志。

Result: 在12012个前瞻性CVE上，预算约束证据选择将泄漏安全的前瞻性recall@50从0.010（仅严重性基线）提升到0.026；每个CVE使用2份证据文档即可捕获大部分价值；跨编码器重排序器反而降低recall至0.016；朴素随机分割使前瞻性recall膨胀8.5倍，EPSS-high recall膨胀5.0倍。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出了泄漏安全的评估协议和可复现的证据证书，用于可争议的漏洞优先级排序声明。语义相关性不等于可利用性证据。
